    82
    83              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_BAUD, 32'd1, 1);
    84
    85                                                   // Keep loopback enabled while IRQ is disabled. The received byte must stay
    86                                                   // in the FIFO without asserting the interrupt.
    87              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 4);
    88              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 4, "reset state with IRQ disabled");
    89              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'ha5, 2);
    90              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 360, "RX pending with IRQ disabled");
    91
    92                                                   // Enabling IRQ while data is already pending must assert it immediately.
    93              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    94              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "enable IRQ with pending data");
    95              1                          3         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    96                                                   if (data[7:0] != 8'ha5) begin
    97              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("IRQ_DATA", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0xa5", data[7:0]))
    98                                                   end
    99              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 8, "FIFO empty after first read");
    100
    101                                                  // With IRQ enabled, a new RX byte must assert it. Disabling IRQ must lower
    102                                                  // the output without consuming the pending byte.
    103             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'h3c, 2);
    104             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 360, "new RX data with IRQ enabled");
    105             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 2);
    106             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 2, "disable IRQ with pending data");
    107             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    108             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "re-enable IRQ with pending data");

    101                                                  // start-bit edge gives nine independent bit-width measurements.

    107             1                        108           measured = this_edge - last_edge;

    109             1                        108                                              : (expected - measured);
